# expected number of segregating sites
ES = function(N, mu, L, n){
  4*N*mu*L*sum( 1/(1:(n-1)) )
}

# reverse a string
strReverse <- function(x) sapply(lapply(strsplit(x, NULL), rev), paste, collapse="")

# HWE heterozygosity at a single site
diversity = function(p){
  1 - (p^2 + (1-p)^2)
}

# fucntion to calculate rates and accuracy
calc_rates = function(truth, test, sample_space){
  
  negatives = sample_space[!(sample_space %in% truth)]
  positives = truth
  
  true_positives = sum(test %in% positives)
  false_positives = sum(!(test %in% positives))
  
  true_negatives = sum(!(negatives %in% test))
  false_negatives = sum(!(positives %in% test))
  
  # accuracy
  acc = (true_positives + true_negatives)/(true_positives + true_negatives + false_positives + false_negatives)
  
  # true positives rate (sensitivity)
  tpr = true_positives/(true_positives + false_negatives)

  # false positive rate (false alarm)
  fpr = false_positives/(false_positives + true_negatives)

  # true negative rate (specificity)
  tnr = true_negatives/(true_negatives + false_positives)

  # false negative rate (miss)
  fnr = false_negatives/(true_positives + false_negatives)

  return(c(length(positives), length(negatives), true_positives, false_positives, true_negatives, false_negatives, acc, tpr, fpr, tnr, fnr))
}

#calc_rates(true_af$pos, varscan$pos, 1:1e6)

# Posterior distribution of allele count i, given minor het-mer count X and total het-mer coverage Z
# n = pool size
# C = average k-mer coverage
# alpha and beta = prior distribution
posterior = function(x,z,n,C,alpha,beta){
  i=1:(n-1)
  p=i/n
  lambdax = i*C
  lambday = (n-i)*C
  
  likelihood_times_prior = (p^(x+alpha-1))*((1-p)^((z-x)+(beta-1)))/((exp(lambdax)-1)*(exp(lambday)-1))
  
  total_probability = sum(likelihood_times_prior)
  
  post = likelihood_times_prior/total_probability
  
  #return(which.max(post)/n)
  return(post)
}

# calculate posterior where the minor k-mer count is variable
truncation_constant = function(c, lambda){
    x = 0:(c-1)
    # truncation constants
    result = 1.0 - sum( (exp(-lambda)*(lambda^x))/factorial(x) )
    return(result)
}

posterior_min_kmer_count = function(x,z,n,C,c,alpha,beta){
  i=c:(n-c)
  #print(i)
  p=i/n
  #print(p)
  lambdax = i*C
  #print(lambdax)
  lambday = (n-i)*C
  #print(lambday)
  
  Tx = unlist(lapply(lambdax, FUN = truncation_constant, c = c))
  #print(Tx)
  Ty = unlist(lapply(lambday, FUN = truncation_constant, c = c))
  #print(Ty)
  
  likelihood_times_prior = (p^(x + alpha - 1))*(1-p)^((z-x) + (beta - 1)) / (Tx*Ty)
  
  total_probability = sum(likelihood_times_prior)
  
  post = likelihood_times_prior/total_probability
  
  return(which.max(post) + c - 1)
  #return(list(which.max(post) + c - 1, post))
}

sterlings_approximation = function(n){
  sqrt(2*pi*n)*((n/exp(1))^n)
}

ln_ster_approx = function(n){
  n*log(n) - n
}

choose_ster_approx = function(z,x){
  z*log(z)-x*log(x)-(z-x)*log(z-x)  
}



pdf_2_ztp = function(z, i, n, N, L, k, G){
  C = ((L - k + 1)/L)*((N*L)/(n*G))

  lambx = i*C

  lamby = (n - i)*C
  
  x = 1:(z-1)
  
  #const = (1/((exp(lambx) - 1)*(exp(lamby) - 1)))

  #sum_terms = ((lambx^x)*(lamby^(z - x)))/(factorial(x)*factorial(z-x))

  const = (1/((exp(lambx) - 1)*(exp(lamby) - 1)*factorial(z)))
  
  #binom = unlist(lapply(x, combn, x = z))
  
  sum_terms = choose(z,x)*(lambx^x)*(lamby^(z - x))
  
  prob = const*sum(sum_terms)

  return(prob)
}

posterior = function(x,z,n,C,alpha,beta){
  i=1:(n-1)
  p=i/n
  lambdax = i*C
  lambday = (n-i)*C
  
  likelihood_times_prior = (p^(x+alpha-1))*((1-p)^((z-x)+(beta-1)))/((exp(lambdax)-1)*(exp(lambday)-1))
  
  total_probability = sum(likelihood_times_prior)
  
  post = likelihood_times_prior/total_probability
  
  return(post)
}

# given a discrete posterior probability distribution, calculate center credible interval
center_credible_interval = function(post, prob,n){
  k = which.max(post)
  total = post[k]
  i = 1
  while(total < prob){
    
    if(k-i < 0){
      left_post = 0
    } else{
      left_post = post[(k-i)]
    }
    
    if(k+i>n){
      right_post = 0
    } else{
      right_post = post[(k+i)]
    }
    
    total = total + left_post + right_post 
    i = i + 1
  }
  
  return(c(k-i,k,k+i))
}

trapezoid <- function(f, x) {
  if (is.function(f) == FALSE) {
    stop('f must be a function with one parameter (variable)')
  }
  total = 0
  for(i in 1:(length(x)-1)){
    a = x[i]
    b = x[(i + 1)]
    h <- b - a
    fxdx <- (h / 2) * (f(a) + f(b))
    total = total + fxdx
  }
  return(total)
}

trapezoid(my_func, p_grid)

results = c()

  likelihood2 <- dbinom(x2, size=z2, prob=p_grid)/(1 - p_grid^z2 - (1 - p_grid)^z2)
  posterior2 <- likelihood2 * prior
  posterior2 <- posterior2/sum(posterior2, na.rm = T)
  results = c(results, p_grid[which.max(posterior2)])
}

# likelihood ratio test
p_null = (x1 + x2)/(z1+z2)

p1 = x1/z1
p2 = x2/z2 

null_likelihood = (dbinom(x1, size=z1, prob=p_null)/(1 - p_null^z1 - (1 - p_null)^z1))*(dbinom(x2, size=z2, prob=p_null)/(1 - p_null^z2 - (1 - p_null)^z2))

alt_likelihood = (dbinom(x1, size=z1, prob=p1)/(1 - p1^z1 - (1 - p1)^z1))*(dbinom(x2, size=z2, prob=p2)/(1 - p2^z2 - (1 - p2)^z2))

lk_ratio_test = -2*log(null_likelihood/alt_likelihood)

pchisq(lk_ratio_test, 1, lower.tail = F)

# z-test
ztest = (p1-p2)/sqrt(p_null*(1-p_null)*(1/(z1) + 1/(z2)))

pnorm(ztest)

total_cov = c()
predictions = c()
for(j in 1:trials){
  x1 = rpois(1, i*C)
  
  x2 = rpois(1, (n-i)*C)

  # iterate through all possible values of i (1 to n-1)
  # find value of i that gives highest probability of observing x1 and x2
  probs1 = dpois(x1, (1:(n-1))*C)
  
  probs2 = dpois(x2, ((n-1):1)*C)

  prediction = which.max(probs1*probs2)

  total_cov = c(total_cov, x1 + x2)
  predictions = c(predictions, prediction)
}

plotdata = data.frame(
  total_cov = total_cov,
  predictions = predictions
)

ggplot(plotdata, aes(y = predictions)) +
  geom_boxplot() +
  geom_hline(yintercept = i, lty = 2) +
  theme_classic()

ggplot(plotdata, aes(y = total_cov)) +
  geom_boxplot() +
  geom_hline(yintercept = n, lty = 2) +
  theme_classic()

# try to get maximum likelihood estimate
# https://stackoverflow.com/questions/59092976/using-optim-for-maximisation
log_likelihood <- function(par, obs) {
  dnbinom(obs, mu = par["mu"], size = par["size"])
}

op_res = optim(c(mu = 25, size = 5), fn = log_likelihood, obs = 43, lower = c(mu = 1, size = 0.01), upper = c(mu = 1000, size = 10000), control = list(fnscale = -1))

op_res$par

Minimum coverage for minor allele frequency

How high does coverage need to be for 95 % of hetmers at a given minor allele frequency to have a kmer count above some threshold?

Even at 200 x coverage, fewer than 95 % of hetmers with minor kmer frequency < 0.01 will have a k-mer count less than 5

i = 2
n = 200
alpha = 0.5
C = 300
L = 150
k = 31

results = expand.grid(
  maf = 1:(n/2),
  cov = seq(from = 50, to = 200, by = 5),
  cutoff = 5
)


results$cdf = qpois(alpha, results$maf/n*results$cov*(L-k+1)/L)

p = plot_ly(
    results, x= ~maf/n, y= ~cov, z= ~cdf,
    type='mesh3d', intensity = ~cdf,
    colors= colorRamp(rainbow(5))
  )

# percent error on diversity
percent_error = function(truth, measurement){
  (measurement - truth)/truth * 100
}

pares$pe_div_kmer = percent_error(pares$true_div, pares$kmer_div)

selective sweeps

process two population metrics (Fst)

simids = params$ID[(params$simtype == "twopop")]

fst = function(n1, c1, f1, n2, c2, f2, type){

  if(type == "sample"){
      pi_wn = (1/2)*( (n1/(n1-1))*(c1/(c1-1))*(1-(f1^2+(1-f1)^2)) + (n2/(n2-1))*(c2/(c2-1))*(1-(f2^2+(1-f2)^2)) )
  }
  
  if(type == "population"){
      pi_wn = (1/2)*( (1-(f1^2+(1-f1)^2)) + (1-(f2^2+(1-f2)^2)) )
  }

  pi_bw = 1 - ( f1*f2 + (1-f1)*(1-f2) )
  
  pi_total = (pi_wn + pi_bw)/2
  
  nei = 1 - pi_wn/pi_total
  
  hudson = 1 - pi_wn/pi_bw
  
  return(data.frame(
    pi_wn = pi_wn,
    pi_bw = pi_bw,
    pi_total = pi_total,
    nei = nei,
    hudson = hudson
  ))
  
}

G = 2e6

k = 31

thresh = 5

#for(i in simids[1:5]){
for(i in 137:160){
  # load in actual allele freqs from simulations
  true_af_p1 = read.table(paste("/mnt/scratch/robe1195/Josephs_Lab_Projects/poolseq-kmers/workflow/slim_allele_freqs_", i, "_p1.txt", sep = ""), col.names = c("chrom", "pos", "ref", "alt", "ns", "af", "ac"))
  
    true_af_p2 = read.table(paste("/mnt/scratch/robe1195/Josephs_Lab_Projects/poolseq-kmers/workflow/slim_allele_freqs_", i, "_p2.txt", sep = ""), col.names = c("chrom", "pos", "ref", "alt", "ns", "af", "ac"))

  true_af_p1$ac = as.numeric(true_af_p1$ac)
  true_af_p2$ac = as.numeric(true_af_p2$ac)
  
  # calculate minor allele frequencies
  true_af_p1$af = as.numeric(true_af_p1$af)
  true_af_p2$af = as.numeric(true_af_p2$af)
  
  true_af_p1$snp_maf = pmin(true_af_p1$af, 1 - true_af_p1$af)
  true_af_p2$snp_maf = pmin(true_af_p2$af, 1 - true_af_p2$af)
  
  true_af_p1 = true_af_p1[(true_af_p1$af < 1) & (true_af_p1$af > 0),]
  true_af_p2 = true_af_p2[(true_af_p2$af < 1) & (true_af_p2$af > 0),]
  
  true_af_p1 = true_af_p1[complete.cases(true_af_p1),]
  true_af_p2 = true_af_p2[complete.cases(true_af_p2),]
  
  true_af = merge(true_af_p1[,c("pos", "ns", "ac", "af")], true_af_p2[,c("pos", "ns", "ac", "af")], by = "pos")
  
  fst_values = fst(2*true_af$ns.x, true_af$ac.x, true_af$af.x, 2*true_af$ns.y, true_af$ac.y, true_af$af.y, "population")
  
  #fst(true_af$ns.x, true_af$ac.x, true_af$af.x, true_af$ns.y, true_af$ac.y, true_af$af.y, "sample")
  
  true_af = cbind(true_af, fst_values)
  
  # load pairs of k-mers
  kmer_pairs_p1 = read.csv(paste("/mnt/scratch/robe1195/Josephs_Lab_Projects/poolseq-kmers/workflow/hetmers_", i, "_p1_seqs.csv", sep = ""), col.names = c("k1", "k2"))

  kmer_covs_p1 = read.csv(paste("/mnt/scratch/robe1195/Josephs_Lab_Projects/poolseq-kmers/workflow/hetmers_", i, "_p1_counts.csv", sep = ""), col.names = c("c1", "c2"))
  
  kmer_hashes_p1 = read.csv(paste("/mnt/scratch/robe1195/Josephs_Lab_Projects/poolseq-kmers/workflow/hetmers_", i, "_p1_hashes.csv", sep = ""), col.names = c("h"))

  kmer_pairs_p1 = cbind(kmer_pairs_p1, kmer_covs_p1, kmer_hashes_p1)

  kmer_pairs_p2 = read.csv(paste("/mnt/scratch/robe1195/Josephs_Lab_Projects/poolseq-kmers/workflow/hetmers_", i, "_p2_seqs.csv", sep = ""), col.names = c("k1", "k2"))

  kmer_covs_p2 = read.csv(paste("/mnt/scratch/robe1195/Josephs_Lab_Projects/poolseq-kmers/workflow/hetmers_", i, "_p2_counts.csv", sep = ""), col.names = c("c1", "c2"))

  kmer_hashes_p2 = read.csv(paste("/mnt/scratch/robe1195/Josephs_Lab_Projects/poolseq-kmers/workflow/hetmers_", i, "_p2_hashes.csv", sep = ""), col.names = c("h"))

  kmer_pairs_p2 = cbind(kmer_pairs_p2, kmer_covs_p2, kmer_hashes_p2)

  # calculate minor allele frequency
  kmer_pairs_p1$kmer_af = kmer_pairs_p1$c1/(kmer_pairs_p1$c1 + kmer_pairs_p1$c2)
  
  kmer_pairs_p2$kmer_af = kmer_pairs_p2$c1/(kmer_pairs_p2$c1 + kmer_pairs_p2$c2)
  
  kmer_pairs_p1p2 = merge(kmer_pairs_p1, kmer_pairs_p2, by = "h")
  
  # map kmers to snps
  pos_kmer_key = read.table(paste("/mnt/scratch/robe1195/Josephs_Lab_Projects/poolseq-kmers/workflow/center_kmer_pairs_", i, "_p1p2.txt", sep = ""), fill = T, col.names = c("pos", "k1", "k2", "k3", "k4"))

  pos_kmer_key$leftk = substr(pos_kmer_key$k1, 1, 15)
  pos_kmer_key$rightk = substr(pos_kmer_key$k1, 17, 31)

  # compare snp and k-mer mafs
  kmer_pairs_p1p2$leftk = substr(kmer_pairs_p1p2$k1.x, 1, 15)
  kmer_pairs_p1p2$rightk = substr(kmer_pairs_p1p2$k1.x, 17, 31)

  pos_kmer_pairs = merge(pos_kmer_key, kmer_pairs_p1p2, by = c("leftk", "rightk"))

  # get snps that pair with reverse complements
  pos_kmer_key$leftk_rc = chartr("ATGC","TACG",pos_kmer_key$leftk)
  pos_kmer_key$rightk_rc = chartr("ATGC","TACG",pos_kmer_key$rightk)

  pos_kmer_key$leftk_rc = strReverse(pos_kmer_key$leftk_rc)
  pos_kmer_key$rightk_rc = strReverse(pos_kmer_key$rightk_rc)

  pos_kmer_pairs_rc = merge(pos_kmer_key, kmer_pairs_p1p2, by.x = c("leftk_rc", "rightk_rc"), by.y = c("rightk", "leftk"))

  pos_kmer_pairs_rc = subset(pos_kmer_pairs_rc, select=-c(leftk,rightk))

  # merge reverse complement and regular sets
  names(pos_kmer_pairs_rc)[1:2] = c("leftk", "rightk")

  pos_kmer_pairs = rbind(pos_kmer_pairs, pos_kmer_pairs_rc)

  # do some filtering
  # remove low coverage k-mer pairs
  # remove tri-allelic k-mers
  #pos_kmer_pairs = pos_kmer_pairs[(pos_kmer_pairs$c1 >=4 & pos_kmer_pairs$c2 >= 4),]
  pos_kmer_pairs = pos_kmer_pairs[( (pos_kmer_pairs$k3 == "") | is.na(pos_kmer_pairs$k3)),]
  
  # calculate bayesian allele counts
  sample_size = params[params$ID == i,"n"]
  
  coverage = params[params$ID == i,"cov"]
  
  sequencer = params[params$ID == i,"sequencer"]
  
  if(sequencer %in% c("miseq","nextseq")){
    L = 300
  }
  
  if(sequencer == "novaseq"){
    L = 150
  }
  
  if(sequencer == "hiseq"){
    L = 125
  }
  
  mean_kmer_coverage_per_genome = ((L-k+1)/L)*(coverage/sample_size)
  
  kmer_bayes_ac_p1 = mapply(posterior_min_kmer_count, pos_kmer_pairs$c1.x, (pos_kmer_pairs$c1.x + pos_kmer_pairs$c1.y), MoreArgs = list(n = sample_size, C = mean_kmer_coverage_per_genome, c = thresh, alpha = 1, beta = 1))
  
  kmer_bayes_ac_p2 = mapply(posterior_min_kmer_count, pos_kmer_pairs$c1.y, (pos_kmer_pairs$c1.y + pos_kmer_pairs$c1.y), MoreArgs = list(n = sample_size, C = mean_kmer_coverage_per_genome, c = thresh, alpha = 1, beta = 1))
  
  # calculate fst for kmers using empirical frequencies
  kmer_fst_values = fst(2*sample_size, pos_kmer_pairs$c1.x, pos_kmer_pairs$kmer_af.x, 2*sample_size, pos_kmer_pairs$c1.y, pos_kmer_pairs$kmer_af.y, "sample")

  pos_kmer_pairs = cbind(pos_kmer_pairs, kmer_fst_values, kmer_bayes_ac_p1, kmer_bayes_ac_p2)
  
  # add in SNP fst values
  snp_vs_kmer_fst = merge(true_af[,c("pos", "nei", "hudson")], pos_kmer_pairs[,c("pos", "nei", "hudson")], by = "pos")
  
  print(ggplot(snp_vs_kmer_fst, aes(x = nei.x, y = nei.y)) +
    geom_point() +
    geom_abline(slope = 1, intercept = 0) +
    theme_classic() +
    labs(x = "true SNP Nei Fst", y = "Nei hetmer Fst", title = i))
  
  print(ggplot(snp_vs_kmer_fst, aes(x = hudson.x, y = hudson.y)) +
    geom_point() +
    theme_classic() +
    geom_abline(slope = 1, intercept = 0) +
    labs(x = "true SNP Hudson Fst", y = "Hudson hetmer Fst", title = i))
  
  # mean(snp_vs_kmer_fst$nei.x)
  # mean(snp_vs_kmer_fst$nei.y)
  # 
  # mean(snp_vs_kmer_fst$hudson.x)

# try to summarize time and memory into snp calling workflow and kmer calling workflow
workflow_summary = function(x, lev_col, resource_col){
  levs = unique(x[,lev_col])
  
  results = NULL
  for(lev in levs){
    bwa = x[(x$tool == "bwa") & (x[,lev_col] == lev),resource_col]
    
    varscan = x[(x$tool == "varscan") & (x[,lev_col] == lev),resource_col]
 
    poolsnp = x[(x$tool == "poolsnp") & (x[,lev_col] == lev),resource_col]

    kmc = x[(x$tool == "kmc") & (x[,lev_col] == lev),resource_col]
    
    smudgeplot = x[(x$tool == "smudgeplot") & (x[,lev_col] == lev),resource_col]
    
    hetmers = x[(x$tool == "hetmers") & (x[,lev_col] == lev),resource_col]

    discosnp = x[(x$tool == "discosnp") & (x[,lev_col] == lev),resource_col]
    
    unitig_caller = x[(x$tool == "unitig_caller") & (x[,lev_col] == lev),resource_col]
    
    result = data.frame(
      lev_col = lev,
      bwa_varscan = bwa+varscan,
      bwa_poolsnp = bwa+poolsnp,
      kmc_smudgeplot = kmc+smudgeplot,
      kmc_hetmers=kmc+hetmers,
      kmc_unitig_caller = kmc+unitig_caller,
      discosnp = discosnp
    )
    
    results = rbind(results, result)
    
  }
  
  names(results) = c("lev_col", "bwa+varscan", "bwa+poolsnp", "kmc+smudgeplot", "kmc+hetmers", "kmc+unitig-caller", "discosnp")
  
  return(results)
}

# calculate error
compare_maf_kmer$error = abs(compare_maf_kmer$kmer_maf - compare_maf_kmer$snp_maf)

summary(compare_maf_kmer$error)

sum(compare_maf_kmer$error < 0.1)/nrow(compare_maf_kmer)

ggplot(confident_sites, aes(x = total_cov, y = error)) +
  geom_point() +
  theme_classic()

#
compare_maf_kmer$snp_maf_bins = cut(compare_maf_kmer$snp_maf, breaks = seq(from = 0, to = 0.5, by = 0.05))
compare_maf_kmer$kmer_maf_bins = cut(compare_maf_kmer$kmer_maf, breaks = seq(from = 0, to = 0.5, by = 0.05))

observed <- table(compare_maf_kmer$kmer_maf_bins)  # Observed frequencies
expected <- table(compare_maf_kmer$snp_maf_bins)  # Expected frequencies

observed = observed[(expected != 0)]
expected = expected[(expected != 0)]

# Calculate Chi-Square statistic manually
chi_sq_statistic <- sum((observed - expected)^2 / expected)
df <- length(observed) - 1
p_value <- 1 - pchisq(chi_sq_statistic, df)

# Print results
print(paste("Chi-Square Statistic:", chi_sq_statistic))
print(paste("Degrees of Freedom:", df))
print(paste("P-value:", p_value))

correlations between true and predicted minor allele frequencies

# load pairs of k-mers
kmer_pairs = read.table(paste("/mnt/scratch/robe1195/Josephs_Lab_Projects/poolseq-kmers/workflow/", "kmerpairs_938_sequences.tsv", sep = ""), col.names = c("k1"))

kmer_covs = read.table(paste("/mnt/scratch/robe1195/Josephs_Lab_Projects/poolseq-kmers/workflow/kmerpairs_938_coverages.tsv", sep = ""), col.names = c("c1", "c2"))

kmer_pairs = cbind(kmer_pairs, kmer_covs)

# calculate minor allele frequency
kmer_pairs$kmer_maf = pmin(kmer_pairs$c1, kmer_pairs$c2)/(kmer_pairs$c1 + kmer_pairs$c2)

# load in actual allele freqs from simulations
true_af = read.table(paste("/mnt/scratch/robe1195/Josephs_Lab_Projects/poolseq-kmers/workflow/slim_allele_freqs_938.txt", sep = ""), col.names = c("chrom", "pos", "af", "ac"))

true_af$ac = as.numeric(true_af$ac)

true_af = true_af[complete.cases(true_af),]

# calculate minor allele frequencies
true_af$af = as.numeric(true_af$af)
true_af$snp_maf = pmin(true_af$af, 1 - true_af$af)

# get key for position to k-mers
pos_kmer_key = read.table(paste("/mnt/scratch/robe1195/Josephs_Lab_Projects/poolseq-kmers/workflow/center_kmer_pairs_938.txt", sep = ""), fill = T, col.names = c("pos", "k1", "k2", "k3", "k4"))

pos_kmer_key$leftk = substr(pos_kmer_key$k1, 1, 15)
pos_kmer_key$rightk = substr(pos_kmer_key$k1, 17, 31)

# compare snp and k-mer mafs
kmer_pairs$leftk = substr(kmer_pairs$k1, 1, 15)
kmer_pairs$rightk = substr(kmer_pairs$k1, 17, 31)

pos_kmer_pairs = merge(pos_kmer_key, kmer_pairs, by = c("leftk", "rightk"))

# get snps that pair with reverse complements
pos_kmer_key$leftk_rc = chartr("ATGC","TACG",pos_kmer_key$leftk)
pos_kmer_key$rightk_rc = chartr("ATGC","TACG",pos_kmer_key$rightk)

pos_kmer_key$leftk_rc = strReverse(pos_kmer_key$leftk_rc)
pos_kmer_key$rightk_rc = strReverse(pos_kmer_key$rightk_rc)

pos_kmer_key$rightk = pos_kmer_key$leftk_rc 
pos_kmer_key$leftk = pos_kmer_key$rightk_rc

pos_kmer_pairs_rc = merge(pos_kmer_key, kmer_pairs, by = c("leftk", "rightk"))

# merge reverse complement and regular sets
any(duplicated(c(pos_kmer_pairs$pos, pos_kmer_pairs_rc$pos)))

pos_kmer_pairs = rbind(pos_kmer_pairs, pos_kmer_pairs_rc[,c(-8,-9)])

# do some filtering
# remove low coverage k-mer pairs
# remove tri-allelic k-mers
pos_kmer_pairs = pos_kmer_pairs[(pos_kmer_pairs$c1 >=4 & pos_kmer_pairs$c2 >= 4),]
pos_kmer_pairs = pos_kmer_pairs[( (pos_kmer_pairs$k3 == "") | is.na(pos_kmer_pairs$k3)),]

compare_maf_kmer = merge(true_af[,c("pos", "snp_maf")], pos_kmer_pairs, by = "pos")

# include varscan calls
varscan = read.table(paste("/mnt/scratch/robe1195/Josephs_Lab_Projects/poolseq-kmers/workflow/calls_938.tsv", sep = ""), header = T)

varscan$snp_maf_varscan = pmin(varscan$Reads1, varscan$Reads2)/(varscan$Reads1 + varscan$Reads2)

names(varscan)[2] = "pos"

# compare varscan and ground truth
compare_maf_varscan = merge(true_af[,c("pos", "snp_maf")], varscan, by = "pos")

# compare k-mer and varscan
compare_maf_kmer_varscan = merge(compare_maf_kmer, varscan, by = "pos")

# round k-mer mafs to nearest increment 
kmer_test = cor.test(compare_maf_kmer$snp_maf, compare_maf_kmer$kmer_maf, method = "pearson")

varscan_test = cor.test(compare_maf_varscan$snp_maf, compare_maf_varscan$snp_maf_varscan, method = "pearson")

# create plots
p1 = ggplot(compare_maf_kmer, aes(x = snp_maf, y = kmer_maf)) +
  geom_point() +
  geom_abline(slope = 1, intercept = 0, lwd = 2, color = "purple") +
  theme_classic() +
  theme(text = element_text(size = 10)) +
  labs(x = "True MAF", y = "MAF from k-mers", title = paste("rho =", signif(kmer_test$estimate, 3), "  p =", paste(format(signif(kmer_test$p.value, 3), digits = 3))))

p2 = ggplot(compare_maf_varscan, aes(x = snp_maf, y = snp_maf_varscan)) +

  theme_classic() +

# try modeling coverage of a k-mer based on it's frequency in a pool
lamb = 60 # average haploid sequencing coverage
rho = 0.8 # dispersion due to sequencing biases
i = 3 # number of genomes where k-mer is present
n = 60 # number of genomes in pool

plotdata = data.frame(
  x = rnbinom(1000, mu = (i/n)*lamb, size = (i/n)*(lamb/rho))
)

ggplot(plotdata, aes(x)) +
  geom_histogram(binwidth = 1) +
  geom_vline(xintercept = (i/n)*lamb)

fitdistr(kmer_counts$c1[], "negative binomial")

slim = read.table("/mnt/scratch/robe1195/Josephs_Lab_Projects/poolseq-kmers/workflow/slim_allele_freqs_840.txt", col.names = c("CHROM", "POS", "snp_af", "ac"))

disco$kmer_min_af = pmin(disco$c1, disco$c2)/(disco$c1 + disco$c2)

slim$snp_min_af = pmin(slim$snp_af, 1 - slim$snp_af)

all_min_af = merge(slim, disco, by = c("CHROM", "POS"), all.x = T, all.y = T)

cor.test(all_min_af$snp_min_af, all_min_af$kmer_min_af, method = "spearman")

  geom_point() +
  theme_classic() +
  geom_abline(slope = 1, intercept = 0)

Test zeta distribution

pA = 0.25
pC = 0.25
pG = 0.25
pT = 0.25
shape_parameter = 2.5
k = 31
L = 1e5

alphabet = c("A", "C", "G", "T")

custom_probs = c(pA, pC, pG, pT)

total_genome = c()

while(k*length(total_genome) < L){

  # generate random k-mer
  kmer = paste(sample(alphabet, size = k, replace = T, prob = custom_probs), collapse = "")

  # detmerine frequency of k-mer
   freq = rzeta(1, shape_parameter)
  #freq = 1

  # copy k-mer 
  kmers = rep(kmer, freq)

  # add k-mers to total_genome
  total_genome = c(total_genome, kmers)
}

# randomly order vector elements
total_genome = sample(total_genome, replace = F)

# concatenate k-mers together
total_genome = paste(total_genome, collapse = "")

# final genome length
nchar(total_genome)

# trim genome to target length
total_genome = substring(total_genome, 1, L)

# final genome length
nchar(total_genome)

# write genome output
#print(total_genome)

# count k-mers in synthetic genome to confirm zipf distribution
start = 1
stop = k

kmer_list = list()

while(stop < L){
  
  kmer = substring(total_genome, start, stop)
  
  if(is.null(kmer_list[[kmer]])){
      kmer_list[[kmer]] = 1
  } else {
    kmer_list[[kmer]] = kmer_list[[kmer]] + 1
  }

  start = start + 1
  stop = stop + 1
}

kmer_freqs = table(unlist(kmer_list))

plotdata = data.frame(
  freqs = as.numeric(names(kmer_freqs)),
  ranks = kmer_freqs
)

#plotdata$ranks.Var1 = as.numeric(plotdata$ranks.Var1)

#plotdata$ranks.Var1 = rank(plotdata$ranks.Freq, ties.method = "min")

ggplot(plotdata, aes(x = log10(freqs), y = log10(ranks.Freq))) +
  geom_point() +
  theme_classic() +
  labs(x = "log10(k-mer count)", y = "log10(Number of k-mers)")

DEPRECATED

kmer_counts = read.table("/mnt/scratch/robe1195/Josephs_Lab_Projects/kmers_poolseq/kmer_counts_1.txt", col.names = c("k1", "c1"))

ggplot(kmer_counts, aes(x = c1)) +
  geom_histogram(binwidth = 1) +
  geom_vline(xintercept = 5) +
  theme_classic()

# loop over k-mers to find ones that putative snps
alphabet = c("A","T","G","C")

results = list()

j = 1
while(nrow(kmer_counts) > 0){
  # get focal k-mer
  focal_kmer = kmer_counts[1,"k1"]
  focal_counts = kmer_counts[1,"c1"]
  print(focal_kmer)

  # generate variant k-mers
  central_base = substr(focal_kmer, 16, 16)

  var_bases = alphabet[!(alphabet %in% central_base)]

  variant_kmers = rep(focal_kmer, times = 3)

  for(i in 1:3){
    var_base = var_bases[i]
    substr(variant_kmers[i], 16, 16) = var_base
  }

  # add reverse complements
  variant_kmers = c(variant_kmers, chartr("ATGC","TACG",variant_kmers))

  # 
  variant_kmers_index = which(kmer_counts$k1 %in% variant_kmers)
  print(variant_kmers_index)

  # store 
  found_variant_kmers = kmer_counts[variant_kmers_index,"k1"]
  found_variant_counts = kmer_counts[variant_kmers_index,"c1"]
  
  if(!identical(found_variant_kmers, character(0))){
      results[[j]] = data.frame(focal_kmer,
                            focal_counts,
                            found_kmers = paste(found_variant_kmers, sep = ";"),
                            found_counts = paste(found_variant_counts, sep = ";"))

    j = j + 1
  }

  # remove focal and variant k-mers from count table
  kmer_counts = kmer_counts[c(-1, -1*variant_kmers_index),]

}
